Left unticked because a review is not a test.

## Possible gaps in the sources (S1 and S2)

These are places where the sources are silent, disagree with the built code, or leave a number open. None of them is written above as a requirement; each needs a decision (and then a checklist item) before the gate of the phase named.

### Stack, repository and tooling
1. **Pinned packages the foundation does not use.** `@nestjs/config` 12.0.1 and `@nestjs/throttler` 6.7.1 are pinned, but the foundation has its own configuration module and rate limiter; `ioredis` is pinned at 6.0.0 but 5.11.1 is installed (S1-STACK-07, -08, -11). The pin table or the code must change.
2. **Node version.** The root `package.json` allows `>=22.12`, the Target architecture says "Node 22/24", the pin says 24 (S1-STACK-01). No source says whether 22 is still supported.
3. **Python tooling and locking.** The Repo layout says the Python service is managed with uv; the README uses venv and pip. The baseline says "exact dependency versions and a committed lockfile — Built", but the fingerprinter's Python dependencies are minimum versions with no lock file.
4. **Slice commands drifted.** The slice verification uses `npm run db:roles / db:migrate / db:seed` and `bench.synth`; the repository has `database:create-roles` and `database:migrate`, no seed command yet, and `bench/synthetic_audio.py` (S2-SEED-05).
5. **Contracts tooling.** "Generated TS types" names no generator; design S2-D8 makes zod the source of truth instead. Nothing says how the Dart side stays in step beyond shared fixtures (S2-CONTRACTS-03, S2-CARDS-23).
6. **Step 2 design decisions not yet in the plan.** Design S2-D1 (versitygw instead of MinIO), S2-D3 (jose instead of `@nestjs/jwt`; 30-second refresh grace; 30-day absolute session), S2-D5 (passwords 15–128 code points), S2-D7 (trusted-device cookie, 20-failure database backstop), S2-D8 (zod contract, ajv only as a test cross-check), S2-D10 (station id in the route path), S2-D11 (same-site production domain), S2-D12 (no `ingest` enqueue in Step 2), S2-D13 (publish needs a verified upload, a card and a schedule, allowed while `PROCESSING`) and S2-D14 (5-minute presigned GET for playback) change or add to the plan. Under "new requirements go into the checklist first", the user should confirm each so the plan and this checklist can be updated.

### Database
7. **The three SECURITY DEFINER functions of plan Step 1** (`mark_ingested`, `resolve`, `record_recognition`) were not built in the Foundation migration; the baseline and the slices put them in Step 3. Step 1 is marked done although its own text lists them.
8. **ADR-002 is missing.** `docs/adr/` does not exist, although ADR-002 (roles and row-level security, with the single-role fallback) describes work built in Step 1 (S1-DOCUMENTS-01).
9. **No proof that the API never changes the schema.** Baseline §1 row 12 has "—" under "Proven by"; a privilege test (no `CREATE` for `lookup_api`) would close it (S1-MIGRATIONS-02).
10. **"All timestamps are `timestamptz NOT NULL`"** cannot hold for optional event times (`archived_at`, `completed_at`, refresh-token revocation time, install `last_seen_at`); the built columns are nullable. The rule probably means "always `timestamptz`; `created_at` and `updated_at` NOT NULL" (S1-SCHEMA-02).
11. **Columns built that no source requires:** `portal_users.full_name` and `status`, `recognition_events.fallback_reason` and its own `idempotency_key` column, `listener_installs.last_seen_at`, unique client names per station, a second index on the hash table, and the Step 2 text-length limits (full name 120, ad title 120, client name 80, campaign name 120). They may be right, but no source states them; the blueprint's `VARCHAR(255)` limits were dropped without replacement.
12. **Unique constraints and row-level security.** Unique checks bypass row-level security, but the plan fixes this only for foreign keys. The global unique email on `portal_users` (and any future global unique on station data) could reveal another station's data through its error; the only stated defence is the leak test.
13. **Roles with undefined rights.** The `SUPPORT` platform role is an allowed value with no stated permissions; the super-admin role's write scope is defined only by the built grants (S1-PRIVILEGES-23).
14. **Who runs scheduled database work.** No phase owns the scheduler for `app.create_recognition_event_partitions()` (S1-SCALING-04), the expired-session clean-up function (S2-SESSIONS-32) or old-partition detaching; the Render topology has no cron service.
15. **Retention and deletion.** No source gives retention periods for audit events, sessions, uploaded ad audio or recognition events, or says how a station, client, ad or user is ever deleted (everything is `RESTRICT`), which matters under the Zambia Data Protection Act.
16. **Local object store hardening.** The baseline requires Postgres and Valkey to listen on 127.0.0.1 with a Valkey password; no source states the same for the object store (MinIO or versitygw) or says where its credentials come from.

### Sign-in and sessions
17. **Numbers not given.** The per-address and per-account sign-in limits, the presigned PUT lifetime, the maximum password length (design: 128), the refresh-reuse grace (design: 30 seconds, 3 uses), the session cap (design: 10) and the absolute session lifetime (design: 30 days) appear in no listed source.
18. **Several stations per user.** The blueprint's token "encodes tenant_id and role permissions"; the plan never says how a user who belongs to several stations picks the station for a session or what `GET /stations/me` returns for them (design S2-D10 answers with the station id in the path).
19. **Password change and reset.** Baseline §3 requires a password change to revoke all sessions (built in the database), but no source describes a password-change screen or endpoint, or a forgotten-password flow.
20. **Session expiry in the portal.** What the portal does when the 15-minute access cookie expires mid-task (silent refresh, return to Sign in, keeping unsaved input) is not stated.
21. **Production cookies across two hosts.** The Render topology has separate `lookup-api` and `lookup-portal` services; two `*.onrender.com` hosts are cross-site, so `__Host-` `SameSite` cookies will not flow between them. The production domain (design S2-D11) is not decided in any source.
22. **Logging of refused requests.** The rules require every 5xx to be logged; no source says whether 4xx answers (repeated 401s, ANALYST 403s, IDOR 404s) are logged and at what level (the code logs them at info), which matters for spotting abuse.

### Uploads, cards and publishing
23. **Upload failure handling.** What `complete` does with a file that fails the first-bytes or size check (refuse and keep `AWAITING_UPLOAD`, or set `FAILED` with a code), and the list of processing error codes with the plain sentence for each, are not stated (S2-PORTAL-ERRORS-03).
24. **Publish rules.** No source says whether an ad can be published before its audio is `READY`, without a card or without windows, or what publish does for `NEEDS_REVIEW` or `FAILED` (design S2-D13 decides it).
25. **The ingest job's shape.** Step 2 creates the job but its contents (presigned GET URL, request id, deadline) are specified only in Step 3; the two steps must agree (S2-ADS-07, S2-LOGGING-04).
26. **How the portal plays private audio.** The list, upload step and detail page all show Play, but no source says how private ad audio is served (design S2-D14: 5-minute presigned GET).
27. **Header image and offer title.** The NewAdButtons wireframe shows "Header image (optional) · Replace" and an "Offer title" (60 characters), but the card contract has no field for either, and no source gives an image upload endpoint, types or size limit.
28. **Action type and field spellings.** `ACTION_LINK`/`ACTION_DIAL`/… (blueprint, plan) against `LINK`/`CALL`/`WHATSAPP`/`MAP` (built), and the blueprint's `PRIMARY_BUTTON`/`header_banner`/`payload` against the plan's `PRIMARY`/`header_image`/semantic fields: the shared contract settles them, but the plan text was not updated.
29. **Abuse of write routes.** Apart from the baseline per-address limit, no source limits ad creation (each returns a presigned URL) or client creation.
30. **Seed content.** The slice walk-through creates an ad for "Brand A", but the seed only says "two clients"; whether one is "Brand A", and the names of the owner's environment variables, are not stated.

### Portal
31. **Screens and states without a wireframe.** The Sign in screen (fields, error text, any "forgot password" link), new-ad step 1 (Client) and step 5 (Review and publish), the ads list's empty, loading and error states, and phone layouts of Ad detail have no wireframe; ad detail exists only at 1280 px (S2-DESIGN-01).
32. **Palette conflict.** Plan §Mobile D asks for "a restrained blue/neutral palette"; the wireframes and `tokens.json` use a warm neutral with a copper accent (#A8431A). Whether the portal ships the dark colours already in `tokens.json` is also not stated.
33. **Touch targets below 44 px in the wireframes** (36 px switches including the 320/411 preview switch, 40 px row buttons, 20 px checkboxes); the plan's 44 px is applied here (S2-ACCESSIBILITY-02), but the sources do not say whether desktop-only controls are exempt.
34. **Portal error reporting (resolved in review round 1).** `POST /api/v1/client-errors` and the portal's reporting were built in Step 2 (`8c69b85`) and now live in S2 (S2-CLIENT-ERRORS). Still open: the portal's offline queue (S2-CLIENT-ERRORS-06) and whether reports are stored anywhere beyond the log (part 3 gap 46).
35. **Request id length.** The portal shows the request id for users to quote (a 36-character UUID); the global rules ask for a "short request id" and the app's Error wireframe shows an 8-character code. No source says how a short code is derived or searched.
36. **Component inventory.** The Design track asks for one but no source says where it lives (S2-DESIGN-02).
37. **Step 2 polling.** The Ad detail polling interval and any back-off are not given (S2-PORTAL-AD-DETAIL-01).

### Tests and evidence
38. **Tests that the built code still lacks** (from `status-built.md` shortfalls): health with the database down, the bypass-role and table-owner start-up branches, the fatal process handlers, migration revert, log redaction of every listed field, the admin and worker roles' attributes and timeouts, the "Fatal error" and `Traceback` leak patterns, the failure count on a wrong password change, and the Postgres index tests that skip without `LOOKUP_FINGERPRINT_TEST_DATABASE_URL`. No continuous integration exists before Phase 8 to make these blocking.
39. **Baseline out of date.** "Every SECURITY DEFINER function pins search_path — Built (one so far)" now covers nine functions; "separate roles … proven by `startup-checks.test.ts`" is wider than the test, which proves only `lookup_api`.
40. **Capture-app tests are manual.** The capture app's flutter tests and the 63-check emulator script are run by hand; no automated suite records their results, and the last emulator run predates commit `8ba386b`, so S1-CAPTURE-APP-01 to -22 were unticked in review round 1 until they are re-run.
